# Installed Claude Skills

This repository has **2560 Claude skills** installed under the `skills/` directory, collected from 67 source repositories plus 5 uploaded skill packs.

Each skill is stored under `skills/<owner>_<repo>/...`, preserving its origin path.

**How skills trigger:** Claude Code skills are *model-invoked*. Claude reads each skill's `description` (in its `SKILL.md` front-matter) and automatically loads the skill when your request matches it. A few are also exposed as `/slash-commands`. The **Trigger** column below summarises each skill's activation cue.

### Source repositories with no installable skills

| Source repo | Note |
|-------------|------|
| [BehiSecc/awesome-claude-skills](https://github.com/BehiSecc/awesome-claude-skills) | Curated "awesome" list (README only) — links to skills hosted in other repos; nothing installable in-repo. |
| [VoltAgent/awesome-agent-skills](https://github.com/VoltAgent/awesome-agent-skills) | Curated "awesome" list (README only) — links out to agent skills; nothing installable in-repo. |
| [jqueryscript/awesome-claude-code](https://github.com/jqueryscript/awesome-claude-code) | Curated "awesome" list (README only) — directory of links; nothing installable in-repo. |
| [deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP](https://github.com/deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP) | A video-generation application (not a Claude skills repo) — no SKILL.md files. |
| [corebunch/Instatic](https://github.com/corebunch/Instatic) | A self-hosted visual CMS application (not a Claude skills repo). Contains 3 SKILL.md files under `.agents/skills/`, but none are installable: `agent-browser` and `skill-creator` are dev-tooling copies of skills already vendored elsewhere in this library (from vercel-labs/agent-browser and the standard Anthropic/Codex skill-creator template, respectively — not original content), and `instatic-user-e2e` is project-internal, referencing `docs/e2e/protocol.md` and other files that only exist inside Instatic's own repo. |
| [ScrapeGraphAI/Scrapegraph-ai](https://github.com/ScrapeGraphAI/Scrapegraph-ai) | A Python scraping library (no SKILL.md). Installed as a package instead: the session-start hook runs `pip install scrapegraphai` in the background. |
| [diegosouzapw/OmniRoute](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute) | An AI gateway application. Its `skills/` are auto-generated API docs for a running OmniRoute server (useless without one, and generic names like `cli-eval`/`omni-inference` would hijack unrelated requests); its `ponytail` skill duplicates DietrichGebert/ponytail already vendored here. Run OmniRoute itself on a persistent machine. |
| [hiteshK03/davinci-resolve-mcp](https://github.com/hiteshK03/davinci-resolve-mcp) | An MCP server that must run on the same machine as DaVinci Resolve — no SKILL.md, and cannot work in a cloud container. Install it locally per its README. |

### Links that could not be installed as skills

- **Google Drive folders** (3 links in the request) — cloud storage, not git repositories; cannot be installed as Claude skills.
